<BlockInfoCard type="sap_concur" color="#FFFFFF" />

Nutzungsanweisungen [#nutzungsanweisungen]

Connect SAP Concur via OAuth 2.0. Manage expense reports and line items, allocations, attendees, comments, exceptions, quick expenses, receipts, travel requests and expected expenses, cash advances, itineraries, user identities, custom lists, budgets, exchange rates, and purchase requests across every Concur datacenter.

Tools [#tools]

sap_concur_approve_expense_report [#sap_concur_approve_expense_report]

Approve an expense report as a manager (PATCH /expensereports/v4/reports/\{reportId}/approve). Required body field: comment.

Eingabe [#eingabe]

| Parameter      | Typ    | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| -------------- | ------ | ------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `datacenter`   | string | Nein         | Concur datacenter base URL (defaults to us.api.concursolutions.com)                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `grantType`    | string | Nein         | OAuth grant type: client\_credentials (default) or password                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `clientId`     | string | Ja           | Concur OAuth client ID                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `clientSecret` | string | Ja           | Concur OAuth client secret                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `username`     | string | Nein         | Username (only for password grant)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `password`     | string | Nein         | Password (only for password grant)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `companyUuid`  | string | Nein         | Company UUID for multi-company access tokens                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `reportId`     | string | Ja           | Expense report ID to approve                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `body`         | json   | Ja           | Request body â€” `comment` is required by Concur (e.g., \{ "comment": "Approved" }). If the report contains rejected expenses, `expenseRejectedComment` is also required. Optional fields: `expectedStepCode`, `expectedStepSequence`, `statusId` (defaults to "A\_APPR"). |

Ausgabe [#ausgabe]

| Parameter | Typ    | Beschreibung                        |
| --------- | ------ | ----------------------------------- |
| `status`  | number | HTTP status code returned by Concur |
| `data`    | json   | Empty (204 No Content)              |
